#### Task M9.5.17b fix round 1 (2026-10-04): rulings T17b-1, T17b-2 and the review's minors

This round supersedes three readings above: the "Salvage numbering" sentence of "The stop and the salvage", the "salvaged on the first pass" sentence of "Restart", and "Addendum (c)"'s last two sentences.

- **T17b-2 (review I1, RR-2).** A lane's racer has exited only through its own `ProcessExited`, or when it never had a process (a launch that failed or that the restart lost: an open racer round with no window). `AgentRound.orphaned` (new, `#[serde(default)]`, skipped while false) marks a racer round that `restore::settle` ended because its window died with the old daemon. `outbox`'s resume of that same round clears it. `race_salvage::kept_because` reads the lane's racer rounds. If a round is open with a window, the lane waits `LANE_EXIT_WAIT_SECS`, then is kept with `its racer did not exit`. If any round is orphaned, the lane is kept at once: `RemoveWorktree { keep_head: true, keep_path: true, clear_locks: false }`, and the run log and the task's history read `race <task>: kept <x> checkout: its racer had no process after the restart`. Otherwise the racer has exited, and the lane is salvaged with `clear_locks` and removed. No kill, signal or scan code changed.
- **m5.** A racer round still open with no window at the restore (its `CreateWindow` lost, awaiting a relaunch that a stopped lane never gets) is not orphaned, because `settle` skips windowless rounds. Its lane is treated as exited, since it never had a process. The kept line above is written only for a lane whose racer had a window. A `CreateWindow` in flight outside a restart is the lane's op (`PendingOp.lane`), so the salvage waits for it.
- **T17b-1.** `edits_state::not_started` is false for a task with a race, whatever its state. `edits_state::started_refusal` gives the started-task text (ruling RR-8) to a pending, queued or blocked race: `add_dep` and an `amend_task` of `deps` get `task <id> has started: its deps cannot change`, `split_task` gets `task <id> has started: it cannot be split`, and a route, size, `test_mode`, `test_mode_reason` or stage amend gets `task <id> has started: its <field> cannot change`. `edits_orch::apply_amend_deps` refuses the same way. `has_started` no longer needs its own `race` clause.
- **m1.** The `cancel_task` edit emits `CancelLive` for any task with a race, not only for a live one, so `race_end::cancel` stops and salvages the lanes of a race blocked after a failed crown, or one whose rounds a restart ended, by the same rules. In the second case the winner had no process after the restart, so its checkout is kept.
- **m2.** `rules::retry` refuses a racing task that is not blocked, and also any racing task with a live lane (`Preparing` through `Review`), whatever its state.
- **m3.** A lane's `salvage_ref` is set only from a successful `Removed` result. A failed salvage leaves it `None`.
- **m4.** `Task.salvage_seq` (new, `#[serde(default)]`, skipped while 0) is the highest salvage number handed out. `merge::reserve_salvage_seq` hands out the next one as each removal is sent, after the highest recorded ref and the counter. It is used for a cancelled task's removal (`dispatch`), each of the merge's three removals, and a stopped lane's salvage. A salvage the restart lost is sent again unchanged (`restore::lost`), so it keeps its number.
- **Accept and discard (verified by reading and by a test).** `complete::run_worktrees` lists, for a raced task, every lane checkout not already listed, with its `.review` and `.proof` checkouts, whatever the lane's state, including a kept one. `checkout_name.rs::accept_and_discard_remove_a_kept_lanes_checkouts` asserts this for both `Accept` and `Discard`.
- **A headless racer's `ProcessExited` comes after the group escalation (verified by reading; no code changed).** `headless/session.rs:268` `kill` sends `SIGTERM` to the group (`:273`). A thread then waits on the leader with `while !*reaped` (`:281`) and sends the group `SIGKILL` at the deadline (`:284-285`). `headless/session/pipes.rs:120` `wait_leader` observes the leader's exit without reaping it (`waitid` with `WNOWAIT`, `:127-132`). Then:
  - while the group id is still the session's, it sends the whole group `SIGKILL` (`:147`);
  - it reaps the leader (`waitpid`, `:151`);
  - it sets `reaped = true` and notifies (`:167-168`), which ends the escalation thread's loop;
  - only then does it send `Msg::Exited` (`:170`).

  The dispatcher delivers `SessionEvent::ProcessExited` after the process's output (`:252`), and the driver maps it to the engine's signal. So a racer's `ProcessExited` means that the leader was reaped, the group was sent `SIGKILL`, and the escalation thread has nothing left to do. The engine's own exit for a window with no process (`run/driver/effects.rs:330-350`, `kill_effect`) has no process to escalate. The guarantee has limits: a descendant that left the process group (`setsid`), or one in uninterruptible sleep, can outlive the group's `SIGKILL`.
- **Tests.**
  - New: `engine/tests/race_end_stops.rs` (9).
  - Added to `race_end_requests.rs`: `a_blocked_race_refuses_deps_splits_and_amends_as_started` and `retry_refuses_a_blocked_race_with_a_live_lane`.
  - Added to `checkout_name.rs`: `accept_and_discard_remove_a_kept_lanes_checkouts`.
  - RED: with every source change reverted to `a9b5a847` and the tests kept, 8 of 12 fail. The other 4 are pinning tests that already passed: the kept-on-both-lanes cancel, the cancel while the winner awaits its crown, the run cancel, and accept and discard.
  - `retry_refuses_a_blocked_race_with_a_live_lane` and the m5 test build their states by hand (the review found no path that reaches them today).

#### Task M9.5.17b fix round 2 (2026-10-04): ruling T17b-3

This round supersedes round 1's m5 reading.

- **N1.** A racer round whose `CreateWindow` the restart lost (`restore::lost`, which sets `relaunch`) is now `orphaned`, because the old daemon may have started that racer. `kept_because` keeps a lane when any of its racer rounds is orphaned, whether or not that round has ended. Such a lane is salvaged with `keep_path` and no lock clearing, and the run log says `its racer had no process after the restart`. Only a launch whose `Failed` result came back counts as exited (`dispatch::launched`). Round 1's m5 test, `a_restart_with_no_racer_window_salvages_the_loser_as_exited`, is removed. Its hand-built state stood for a lost launch, and that case is now kept (`a_losers_launch_lost_at_a_restart_keeps_its_checkout`).
- **N2.** `orphaned` is cleared in `outbox`'s `Resumed` arm, not when the `ResumeSession` is sent. A `ResumeFailed` or `Failed` resume ends the round and leaves it orphaned. The lane's checkout is then kept, even after the fresh session that takes over the lane exits (`a_racer_whose_resume_failed_keeps_its_checkout`).
- **A relaunch (beyond the ruling, by the same rule as N2).** A relaunched round's `Window` result clears `orphaned` (`dispatch::launched`). The new window's `ProcessExited` is then the racer's own (`a_relaunched_racer_is_no_longer_orphaned`). An old daemon's racer that survives a crash goes untracked after a successful resume or relaunch. That limit applies to every task and is pre-existing; it is in the followups.
- **N3.** The doc comment of `Batch::refuse` is back on `refuse`.
- **N4.** `rules::has_live_lane` calls `race_view::live`.
- **Escalation reading, the `ECHILD` branch.** When `waitid` fails with anything but `EINTR` (`pipes.rs:139-143`), something else reaped the leader, so its pid may already belong to another process. `wait_leader` then signals nothing and reaps nothing (`:164-166`). It still sets `reaped = true` (`:167`), which ends the escalation thread's loop, and sends `Msg::Exited` (`:170`). In that branch `ProcessExited` therefore arrives with no group `SIGKILL` from `wait_leader`. The escalation thread's own `SIGKILL` is sent only if its deadline came first. The anthrex daemon reaps its session children only here, so this needs an outside reaper. It is pre-existing and is listed beside the `setsid` and uninterruptible-sleep limits. No kill code changed.
- **Tests.** There are 3 new tests in `race_end_stops.rs`. All 3 failed before the fix, each on its own assertion:
  - the lost-launch test got `clear_locks: true`;
  - the relaunch test found the round not orphaned while its relaunch was in flight;
  - the failed-resume test got `clear_locks: true`.

  With the fix, the daemon library passes 3073 of 3073.

### Task M9.5.18 (2026-10-04): launching racers and test writers

The brief's section and the controller's addendum, with these readings and deviations:

- **`--lane` only for a racer** (task 2 review m2). `headless/argv.rs::mcp_args` writes `--lane` only when the target's role is `Racer`; a lane left on any other role's target is dropped, since `anthrex mcp` refuses it. Test: `argv_lane_tests.rs::only_a_racer_names_its_lane` (RED: a worker target wrote `--lane a`).
- **The racer's prompt (ruling T1-3) needed no prompt code.** Since task 17a, a racer's first turn (`worker_prompt`, or `handover_prompt` for a fresh session) is written by `dispatch_session::launch` inside the lane's view, whose task already has the lane's `worktree` and `branch` (`race_view.rs`). `racer_spec` builds only the `HeadlessSpec`, not the prompt, so "racer_spec passes worker_prompt a copy" does not describe the code; its copy of the task now also carries the lane's `branch` (`task_branch(run, lane.checkout)`) so the two agree, which changes no output. `engine/tests/race_launch.rs::a_racers_prompt_names_its_lanes_checkout_and_branch` is therefore a pinning test (it passed before any change): through the reducer, lane a's and lane b's first turns name `<wt>/runs/<id>/t1.<x>` and `anthrex/<id>/t1.<x>`, every other line equals the unraced task's `worker_prompt`, and no line says "race". `run/contract.rs` is unchanged.
- **`a_racer_mcp_config_names_its_lane`** (`role_launch_patterns_tests.rs`, new) is a pinning test: `racer_spec`'s Claude `--mcp-config` and Codex `-c mcp_servers.anthrex.args` already carried task 2's `--lane`.
- **`ToolCall.lane` and `observe.rs`.** `observe.rs` translates the manager's window signals and never sees a tool call, so it is unchanged. A call goes `anthrex mcp` → `mcp::forward` (sets `lane`, task 2) → the socket → `RunService::tool` → `EventKind::Tool { call }` untouched; the engine routes it by its window (`race::lane_of_window`). `driver/racer_launch_tests.rs::a_racers_tool_call_carries_its_lane` forwards a racer's `task_done` over a real daemon socket and reads the engine's queue directly: the call arrives with `role: Racer`, `lane: Some(A)`. It is a pinning test.
- **Sandboxes (task 15's addendum and re-review N3).** `driver/ops.rs::worker_git_dirs` now completes the sandbox of every `writes_task` role (worker, test writer, racer), each on `lane_ops::session_checkout`: a racer's lane's stored `Lane.checkout`, else the task's `checkout_name()`. `lane_ops::checkout_of` returns `Result`: a lane the run stores no `Lane` for is an error (`task <id> has no lane <x>; its racer gets no sandbox roots`), never the task's own checkout, and a racer whose `RunRef` names no lane is refused too (`a racer of task <id> names no lane; it gets no sandbox roots`). Either refusal is logged (`tracing::error!`, "a racer's launch was refused") and returned before any directory is made, so the `CreateWindow` fails and the engine's existing path blocks the lane with `could not start the session: <text>`. The refusal applies whether or not the session is sandboxed. Nothing was widened: a racer's grant is exactly a worker's grant computed for its own lane's checkout. Tests (`driver/racer_launch_tests.rs`, real git, three standalone checkouts):
  - `a_racers_sandbox_covers_its_own_lanes_git_dir_only`: lane a (Claude) and lane b (Codex) each get their own objects directory and their own git directory's `index`, and nothing under the other lane's repository or checkout, the task's own repository or checkout, the user's repository or its git common directory. RED: the racer's roots were left unresolved (no git directory files).
  - `a_test_writers_sandbox_is_its_tasks_checkout`. RED: the test writer's roots were left unresolved.
  - `a_lane_with_no_stored_entry_is_refused_its_launch`: the spec's roots are unchanged and `tasks/t1/git/objects` is never made for lane b. RED: `Ok(())`.
  - `lane_ops_tests.rs::a_racers_checkout_is_its_lanes_stored_one` follows the new `Result`.
- **`fake-agent`** (decision 31). `roles::key` gives a racer the key `<task>-<lane>` (from `--task` and `--lane`), so its script is `racer-<task>-<lane>-<n>.jsonl` and `claim`'s prefix `racer-t1-a-` never matches lane b's. A test writer's key was already its `--task` (`test_writer-<task>-<n>.jsonl`); `headless.rs` needed no change. Tests (`fake-agent/tests/pattern_roles.rs`, new): `racer_scripts_are_claimed_by_lane` (lane b first, then lane a first; RED: lane b's racer claimed nothing) and `test_writer_scripts_are_claimed` (in order of `n`, the worker's script left for the implementer; pinning).
- **Sizes:** `driver/ops.rs` 552→562 (+10), `driver/lane_ops.rs` 127→151, `role_launch_patterns.rs` 48→56 (incl. the test registration), `headless/argv.rs` 479→480, `fake-agent/src/roles.rs` 373→384 (+11 of +30), `fake-agent/src/headless.rs` 571 (unchanged). New tests: `driver/racer_launch_tests.rs` 346, `role_launch_patterns_tests.rs` 62, `engine/tests/race_launch.rs` 57, `fake-agent/tests/pattern_roles.rs` 93; `headless/argv_lane_tests.rs` 59→84.

### Task M9.5.19 (2026-10-04): the output filter for Codex workers

Decision 28, instruction path only, with these readings and deviations:

- **The Codex hook facts are not recorded.** Task M9.5.1's items 4–6 are still "for the user to run": nothing under them records a probe's output. So the brief's fallback holds, and `CLI_CAPS.codex_filter` is `CodexFilter::Instruction`: **codex output filter: by instruction only (the CLI cannot rewrite commands).** Everything the brief makes conditional on those facts is left out: `output_filter::codex_rewrite`, `codex_hook_args`, `default_hook_source`, the `CodexFilter::Hook` variant, `CliCaps.codex_shell_tool_names` and `codex_command_pointer`, `anthrex filter-hook --runtime codex` (`cli/src/filter_hook.rs` is untouched), `cli/tests/filter_hook_codex.rs`, and the seven hook-only tests, `no_hook_source_means_instruction` included. `CodexFilter` therefore has the one variant; its doc names what joins it with the facts. Nothing passes `--dangerously-bypass-hook-trust`.
- **What changed.** `role_launch.rs::worker_spec` sets `output_filter` for both runtimes (the `.filter(|_| claude)` is gone), under the same conditions (stored profile, a filtering mode, a prefix). Racers and test writers are built on `worker_spec` (`role_launch_patterns.rs`), so they inherit it with their own checkout's log directory, and `role_launch_patterns.rs` needed no change. `codex_args` writes `developer_instructions` from `output_filter::codex_instructions`: the spec's instructions, then, when the spec carries a filter hook, a blank line and `codex_filter_note`, on the first turn and on every resumed one. Reviewers, scouts, planners, deciders and orchestrators carry no hook, so their instructions are unchanged. Nothing is added to a Codex worker's writable roots: the log directory is `task_tmp_dir(data, checkout_name)/anthrex-logs`, under one of `worker_git_roots`, and `filter-run` creates it.
- **`codex_instructions` lives in `output_filter.rs`, not `argv.rs`**, to keep `argv.rs` inside its budget (480→497 against 499).
- **Quoting in the note.** `<exe>` and `<log_dir>` are shell-quoted as `wrap` and `hook_command` quote them (`'/path/anthrex' … --log-dir '/path/anthrex-logs'`), so the command the note names runs as written when a path has a space. The rest of the text is the Interfaces row word for word, with `<command>` left as the literal placeholder inside its quotes.
- **Tests.** RED was taken against stubs (an empty `codex_filter_note` and no wiring), after a first compile-only RED.
  - `output_filter_tests.rs::codex_filter_note_is_the_exact_text` (new, beyond the list): the exact text, with two prefixes, the `tail` mode and a log directory with a space. RED: `""`.
  - `argv_filter_tests.rs::codex_args_put_the_filter_note_after_the_instructions` (new, beyond the list): `CLI_CAPS.codex_filter == Instruction`; a Codex spec with a hook gets `instructions + "\n\n" + note`, new and resumed, and nothing else on the argv changes. RED: the plain instructions. `reviewers_scouts_and_codex_get_no_filter_hook` keeps its Claude half and now asserts only that a Codex argv carries no `filter-hook` (its old "the argv is unchanged" half was the behaviour this task replaces).
  - `role_launch_filter_tests.rs::a_codex_worker_gets_the_instruction_note`: a Codex worker and a Codex test writer get the note after their own contract, the log directory their `TMPDIR`'s `anthrex-logs`; lane b's racer (Codex) gets lane b's (`t1.b`); lane a's racer (Claude) gets the hook and no note. RED: no note. `worker_spec_sets_the_hook_only_for_a_stored_profile_with_prefixes` now asserts a Codex worker's hook equals a Claude worker's (RED: `None`).
  - `the_log_dir_is_already_a_codex_writable_root` (**pinning**, passed before the change): the roots equal `worker_git_roots`, contain `TMPDIR`, contain the log directory, are the same with no filter, and for lane b's racer are lane b's.
  - `reviewers_and_scouts_never_get_it` also passed before the change (a guard that the change does not spread): Claude and Codex reviewers and area scouts have no hook, and a Codex reviewer's or scout's `developer_instructions` is its contract alone.
- **Sizes:** `output_filter.rs` 306→344 (+38 of +70), `headless/argv.rs` 480→497, `run/role_launch.rs` 391→393, `output_filter_tests.rs` 306→329, `argv_filter_tests.rs` 71→109, `role_launch_filter_tests.rs` 107→307.
